others 0.

Term minus (k=-d): grad_left += w_minus[d]*(1-h_right); grad_right -= w_minus[d]*h_left.
Combine:
grad_left += w_minus[d]*(1-h_right) - w_plus[d]*h_right
= w_minus[d] - (w_plus[w? no] h_right) - w_minus[d] h_right? Wait w_minus*(1 - h_right) - w_plus*h_right = w_minus - (w_plus + w_minus)*h_right.
grad_right += w_plus[d]*(1-h_left) - w_minus[d]*h_left = w_plus - (w_plus + w_minus)*h_left.
For d=0: grad += w0*(1-2h). But also from plus/minus same? not.

w = diff weights.

grad[d:n] += w_plus[d] - (w_plus[d]+w_minus[d])*h[0:n-d]

Local pair polishing: We can implement efficient stochastic pair descent using gradient.
Algorithm `pair_polish(h,n,deadline,step_schedule)`:
- h projected, f=compute_c5.
- For several base step sizes (0.02 down to 1e-4):
  - For iteration until no improvement or time:
    - compute c, g = active/smooth gradient (with high temp maybe exact active)
    - If f near, maybe break.
    - Construct candidate pairs:
       * Let low = indices of smallest g (size K), high = largest g. K ~ 80. Candidate all pairs low/high? 6400 too many. Instead choose top mixed: for t in range(K): pair (low[t], high[-1-t]) maybe 80. Also local pairs: for i sample, pair with i+1? Or use precomputed adjacent/near for fine.
       * Also random pairs based on gradient difference: sample pairs? Need overall.

Projection function bisection:
```
def project_feasible(x,n):
    target=n/2.0
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float)
    # if sum close and in bounds return clip?
    if x.min()>=0 and x.max()<=1 and abs(x.sum()-target)<1e-12:
        return np.clip(x,0,1)
    lo = x.min() - 1.0
    hi = x.max()
    for _ in range(80):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       s=np.clip(x-mid,0,1).sum()
       if s>target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    p=np.clip(x-(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
    # adjust last to fix roundoff
    diff=target-p.sum()
    if diff !=0:
       # add to variable with room or subtract
       idx = np.where((p<1)&(p>0) or (diff>0 and p<1) or (diff<0 and p>0))[0]
       if len(idx): p[idx]+=diff/len(idx)
    return np.clip(p,0,1)
```
Need handle if no idx; but usually.

Can compute C vector:
```
def c5_vec(h,n):
   dx=2.0/n
   return np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')*dx
```
`compute_c5 = float(np.max(c5_vec))`.

Weights:
```
def make_weights(c, beta):
   cm=c.max()
   z=beta*(c-cm)
   # clip z >? if beta large, exp(z) for max=1; softmax stable: exp(z - zmax)
   zmax=z.max()
   w=np.exp(z-zmax)
   s=w.sum()
   if s>0: w/=s
   return w, cm
```

Gradient formula:
For d>0:
For left positions r (0..n-d-1): g_r += wm[d] - (wp[d]+wm[d]) h_{r+d}
For right positions r (d..n-1): g_r += wp[d] - (wp[d]+wm[d]) h_{r-d}
This is like for each d, add constant (depends d) to parts and convolution of h with a sequence A[d] = wp[d]+wm[d]. Specifically g_r -= ∑_{d: r>=d?} (wp[d]+wm[d]) h_{r-d} + ∑_{d: r+d<n?} (wp[d]+wm[d]) h_{r+d} + constants from wm for right? Let's derive:
Term -a_d h_{r+d} for r< n-d (from plus? d as lag plus: grad left) + -a_d h_{r-d} for r>=d (from grad right). So h part: `- ∑_{d=1}^{r} a_d h_{r-d} - ∑_{d=1}^{n-1-r} a_d h_{r+d}`.
Constant part: For d where r< n-d (always for all d? if r <= n-d-1), plus left constant wm[d]. Also for d where r>=d, right constant wp[d]. So constant = ∑_{d=1}^{n-1-r} wm[d] + ∑_{d=1}^{r} wp[d]. (Check for right term constant added to positions r>=d: +wp[d]). So:
g_r = w0(1-2h_r) - ∑_{d=1}^{r} a_d h_{r-d} - ∑_{d=1}^{n-1-r} a_d h_{r+d} + ∑_{d=1}^{r} wp[d] + ∑_{d=1}^{n-1-r} wm[d], times dx.
This can be computed via convolutions! a length n. The terms are autocorrelation-like of h with a and trailing sums of weights.

Potential improvements to projection:
```
def project_feasible(x,n):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float).ravel()
    target=0.5*n
    if x.size!=n: return x
    if x.min()>=0 and x.max()<=1:
       diff=target-x.sum()
       # if diff small add uniformly? But to maintain box:
       if abs(diff)<1e-12: return x
       if diff>0:
          room=1-x; tot=room.sum()
          if tot>0: x=x+diff*room/tot
       else:
          tot=x.sum()
          if tot>0: x=x*(1+diff/tot) ? Actually subtract proportional: x = x - (-diff)*x/tot = x*(1+diff/tot)
       return clip
    # bisection for box+sum
    lo=x.min()-1; hi=x.max()
    for _ in range(70):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       if np.clip(x-mid,0,1).sum() > target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    p=np.clip(x-(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
    # fix residual
    diff=target-p.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-15:
       if diff>0: candidates=(p<1)
       else: candidates=(p>0)
       if candidates.sum()>0: p[candidates]=np.clip(p[candidates]+diff/candidates.sum(),0,1)
    return p
```
The L2 projection actually p=clip(x-λ). Good. Need ensure if x has sum target but out of bounds, bisection. If x all ones and target less, lambda works.

But after L-BFGS with bounds, x within [0,1], sum near; faster branch can adjust proportionally. Need preserve box. For diff>0, adding proportional to room maintains max <=1? `x + diff*room/tot`; if room some 0, x+... ≤ x + diff*(1-x)/tot. Could a component exceed 1? Since diff ≤ tot (target<=n), scale ≤1. For x=0.9, room=0.1, scale maybe 1, x_new=1.0. okay. For diff<0: `x + diff*x/tot`? diff negative, scale = 1 + diff/tot. diff >= -target = -tot? scale >=0. x_new between 0 and x. Good. Active mask selection:
```
def active_weights(c, temp):
    mx=c.max()
    mask = c >= mx - temp
    if not mask.any(): mask[int(np.argmax(c))]=True
    w=np.zeros_like(c); w[mask]=1.0; w/=w.sum()
    # optionally add small uniform
    # w = (1-eps)*w + eps/len(c)
    return w
```

def pair_polish(h,n,deadline, base_sizes=(0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001,0.0005,0.0002,0.0001), K=80):
    h=project(h); best=compute; 
    for base in base_sizes:
      improved_any=True
      while improved_any and time<deadline:
        improved_any=False
        c=c5_vec(h)
        w=active_weights(c, max(1e-5, best*1e-6))? Actually active temp = max(1e-4, 1e-6)
        g=grad_weights(w,h,n)
        # maybe normalize differences: sort indices by g.
        order=np.argsort(g)
        lows=order[:K]; highs=order[n-K:]
        # candidate pairs list maybe lows/highs limited. Also adjacent: for i in random/sample, pairs (i,i+1)
        # Try local neighborhood pairs: for d=1..R, for i sample? Could exhaustive for n=300, R=20: 5800 pairs; one eval each maybe okay? But repeated.
```

Pseudo:
```
def pair_polish(h,n,deadline, base_sizes=(...)):
   best=compute
   while time<deadline:
      c=c5_vec; w=active; g=grad
      if isclose break
      order=np.argsort(g)
      K=min(80,n//2)
      lows=order[:K]; highs=order[n-K:][::-1]
      candidates=[]
      for a,b in zip(lows,highs): candidates.append((a,b))
      # also if n small add random? deterministic seed? Use rng to add 100 random pairs.
      # local: for i in 0..n step max(1,int(n/100)), for d in 1..5: (i, (i+d)%? no wrap? No wrap, only indices in [0,n). Could add.
      snapshot=h.copy(); sbest=best; gain=1e-15; best_pair=None; best_delta=None
      for i,j in candidates_unique:
         if i==j continue
         # choose two directions: dpos: increase i decrease j if g[i]<g[j]? Actually derivative (g_i - g_j)*δ; to decrease, choose δ sign = -sign(g_i-g_j). If g_i<g_j, increase i (δ>0). If g_i>g_j, decrease i (δ<0).
         if g[i] < g[j]:
            maxpos = min(1-h[i], h[j]); if maxpos<=0 continue; delta=min(base,maxpos)
            # Also maybe try delta=maxpos? If small. Use halving if first not improve? We'll just try delta and maybe delta*0.5?
         else:
            maxpos = min(h[i], 1-h[j]); delta = -min(base,maxpos)
         # evaluate h_i+delta, h_j-delta
         h[i]+=delta; h[j]-=delta
         cnew=compute_c5(h,n)
         h[i]-=delta; h[j]+=delta
         if cnew < sbest - 1e-14:
            gain=sbest-cnew; best_pair=(i,j,delta)
      if best_pair:
          apply; sbest=cnew; improved=True
          if sbest <= best-target? update
      if not improved:
          # try smaller base? break base loop
          break
   return h,best
```

def random_h(n, seed, base=None, amp=0.05):
  rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
  h=np.full(n,0.5)
  for k in 1..6:
    freq=rng.randint(1,n//4); phase=rng.uniform(0,2π)

Let's detail active LBFGS pipeline.

```
def lbfgs_helpers(...):
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    target=0.5*n
    def obj_w(x,n,w,mu,deadline):
       if deadline and time.time()>deadline: raise RuntimeError
       c=c5_vec(x,n)
       val=float(np.dot(w,c))
       if mu: val += mu*(x.sum()-target)**2
       return val
    def grad_w(x,n,w,mu,deadline):
       if deadline...
       g=grad_weights(w,x,n)
       if mu:
           d=x.sum()-target
           g += 2.0*mu*d*np.ones(n) # allocating each time; use np.full? okay
       return g
```
But nested functions violate "no closures or lambdas"? It says Make all helper functions top level, no closures or lambdas. So cannot define obj/grad inside optimize. We must top-level with args. Fine.

def lbfgs_minimize(x, objfun, gradfun, args, n, maxiter, deadline):
   bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
   try:
      res=minimize(objfun, x, jac=gradfun, args=args, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={...})
      y=project_feasible(res.x,n)
      return y
   except Exception:
      return x
```

SLSQP:
```
def slsqp_opt(h,n,beta,deadline,maxiter):
   bounds=[(0,1)]*n
   cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':eq_fun,'jac':eq_jac,'args':(n,)}]
   try: res=minimize(obj_lse,h,args=(n,beta,deadline),jac=grad_lse,method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-14})
   except: return h
   return project(res.x,n)
```

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100):
 t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-3
 # get initial
 try: h= np.array(initial_h_values,float).copy(); n=len(h)

Let's refine gradient vectorization with optional skip for zero weights.
```
def grad_weights(w,h,n):
    g=np.zeros(n,float)
    mid=n-1
    w0=w[mid]
    if w0 != 0.0:
        g += w0*(1.0-2.0*h)
    wp_plus=w[mid+1:]
    wm_minus=w[:mid][::-1]
    # find nonzero d
    nz = np.nonzero((abs(wp_plus)>0) | (abs(wm_minus)>0))[0]+1
    # if nz large, use range? np.nonzero cost. For softmax many nonzero. Could threshold tiny 1e-300? Most small underflow? For beta moderate many >1e-16.
    for d in nz:
       wp=wp_plus[d-1]; wm=wm_minus[d-1]; a=wp+wm
       if a == 0: continue
       left=n-d
       g[:left] += wm - a*h[d:]
       g[d:] += wp - a*h[:left]
    dx=2.0/n
    return g*dx
```
If we skip very small weights, gradient approximate. For L-BFGS smooth objective, weights from softmax include tiny terms; skipping <1e-15 might be okay but derivative not exact, could affect convergence. Use threshold 0.0. `np.nonzero` with all nonzero may be 300. If many, loop. For active with few, fast. For LSE with beta small (e.g., 1), weights broad; loop all. Fine.

Potential overflow in softmax for beta huge: `z=beta*(c-mx)` <=0, no underflow issue; exp(z-zmax) with zmax=0 (since max c gives 0) so exp(0)=1. Good. If due c max multiple, zmax=0. If all c nan? no.

`lse_value`:
```
z=beta*(c-mx)
zmax=z.max() # 0
s=np.sum(np.exp(z-zmax))
val=mx + (zmax + np.log(s))/beta # zmax 0
```
If beta=0? Use active? We'll not use beta=0 in LSE. If beta <1, logsumexp okay.

```
def random_perturb(h, n, seed, amp):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    x=np.linspace(0,2,n)
    h2=h.copy()
    # add low-freq smooth noise to keep plausible
    coeffs = rng.uniform(-1,1,size=5)
    phases = rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi,size=5)
    for k in range(1,6):
       h2 += amp/(k+1)*coeffs[k-1]*np.sin(np.pi*k*x/2+phases[k-1])
    return project_feasible(h2,n)
```

  y=(h>th).astype(float)
  return project_feasible(y,n)
```

Let's write `optimize_general` robust:
```
def optimize_general(h, n, deadline, quality=2):
    h = project_feasible(h,n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
    cur_h=h.copy(); cur_c=best_c
    temps = (0.03,0.01,0.003,0.001,0.0003,0.0001) if quality==2 else (0.01,0.001,0.0001)
    if n > 350:
       mus=(100,1000); maxit=50
    else:
       mus=(100,1000); maxit=120
    for temp in temps:
       if time.time() > deadline - 15: break
       c_cur=c5_vec(cur_h,n)
       for mu in mus:
          if time.time() > deadline - 10: break
          w=active_weights(c_cur,temp)
          val_cur=float(w.dot(c_cur)) + mu*(cur_h.sum()-n/2)**2
          cur_h = lbfgs_minimize(cur_h, n, w, mu, maxit, deadline)
          c_new=c5_vec(cur_h,n)
          val_new=float(w.dot(c_new)) + mu*(cur_h.sum()-n/2)**2
          # accept if better current C or active objective and slight increase
          if c_new < cur_c - 1e-12 or (val_new < val_cur - 1e-9 and c_new < cur_c + 3e-6):
              cur_c = c_new
              if cur_c < best_c:
                  best_c=cur_c; best_h=cur_h.copy()
          else:
              # revert to previous best/current? We don't have old cur_h. Need keep old copy.
```

```
def lbfgs_minimize(h,n,w,mu,maxit,deadline):
   y=project_feasible(h,n)
   try:
      res=minimize(obj_weighted,y,args=(n,w,mu,deadline), jac=grad_weighted, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=[(0,1)]*n, options=...)
      return project_feasible(res.x,n)
   except Exception:
      return None
```

Let's write pair_polish carefully.
```
def pair_polish(h,n,deadline,base_sizes=(0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001,0.0005,0.0002,0.0001), max_rounds=500):
    h=project_feasible(h,n).copy()
    best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
    best_h=h.copy()
    rng=np.random.RandomState(12345)
    for base in base_sizes:
       improved=False
       rounds=0
       while time.time()<deadline and rounds<max_rounds:
           # recompute c,w,g
           c=c5_vec(h,n); cm=c.max()
           if cm < best_c - 1e-13: best_c=cm; best_h=h.copy()
           w=active_weights(c, max(1e-5, 1e-6*base?)) maybe temp=1e-4
           g=grad_weights(w,h,n)
           # if max gradient diff small break? 
           order=np.argsort(g)
           K=min(70, n//3)
           lows=order[:K]
           highs=order[n-K:][::-1]
           # candidate array
           cands=[]
           seen=set()? Set of tuples overhead. Use list and check i==j.
           for a,b in zip(lows, highs): cands.append((a,b))
           # add adjacent/local: for i in range(0,n, max(1,n//100)): for d in 1..3 append i,i+d
           step_range=max(1,n//100)
           for i in range(0,n,step_range):
               for d in (1,2,3):
                   j=i+d
                   if j<n: cands.append((i,j))
           # maybe random 50
           for _ in range(40):
               i=rng.randint(n); j=rng.randint(n); if i!=j cands.append((i,j))
           snapshot=h.copy()
           best_pair=None; best_gain=best_c - c approx? 
           for (i,j) in cands:
               if i==j: continue
               diff = g[i] - g[j]
               if abs(diff) < 1e-12: continue
               if diff < 0: # increase i, decrease j
                   delta = min(base, 1-h[i], h[j])
               else:
                   delta = -min(base, h[i], 1-h[j])
               if delta ==0: continue
               h=snapshot; h[i]+=delta; h[j]-=delta
               cn=compute_c5(h,n)
               if cn < best_c - 1e-14:
                   gain=best_c-cn
                   best_pair=(i,j,delta)
                   best_c? use current best? Use current base snapshot max = c.max() maybe.
           if best_pair:
              apply to snapshot? We need after loop, use snapshot again:
              i,j,d=best_pair; h2=snapshot; h2[i]+=d; h2[j]-=d; h2=project? Sum preserved, bounds okay. c2=compute; h=h2; best_c=c2; best_h=h.copy(); improved=True; rounds+=1
           else:
              break
       if not improved:
           continue
    # final ensure best_h
    return best_h,best_c
```
This is a bit complex. We can simplify: local variable `cur_c = c5_vec(h,n).max()`. In candidate eval, compare to `cur_c` (snapshot max), not global best. But global best_h updates. We can track `cur_c` as max c of current h; after each candidate compute relative.

```
def pair_polish(h,n,deadline,bases=(...),max_rounds=300):
    h=project_feasible(h,n).copy()
    cur_c=compute_c5(h,n); best_c=cur_c; best_h=h.copy()
    rng=np.random.RandomState(98765)
    for base in bases:
      rounds=0
      while time.time() < deadline and rounds < max_rounds:
        c=c5_vec(h,n); cm=float(c.max())
        if cm < cur_c - 1e-14: cur_c=cm; if cm<best_c: update
        w=active_weights(c, max(1e-5, base*0.1?)) # fixed 1e-4 maybe
        g=grad_weights(w,h,n)
        order=np.argsort(g)
        K=min(60,n//3)
        lows=order[:K]; highs=order[-K:][::-1]
        # candidate generation using list maybe max 200
        candidates=[]
        add = lambda? no lambda. Top-level helper? Could inline loops.
        for t in range(K): candidates.append((lows[t], highs[t]))
        st=max(1,n//80)
        for i in range(0,n,st):
            for d in (1,2,3,5):
                j=i+d
                if j<n: candidates.append((i,j))
        for _ in range(50): ...
        # deduplicate? Could skip for speed; duplicate eval okay.
        snap=h.copy()
        chosen=None; chosen_c=cur_c; chosen_gain=1e-15
        for pair in candidates:
           i,j=pair
           if i==j: continue
           diffg=g[i]-g[j]
           if diffg < 0:
              delta=min(base, 1.0-h[i], h[j])
           else:
              delta=-min(base, h[i], 1-h[j])
           if delta == 0: continue
           snap[i]+=delta; snap[j]-=delta
           cval=compute_c5(snap,n)
           snap[i]-=delta; snap[j]+=delta
           if cval < chosen_c - 1e-14:
              chosen=(i,j,delta); chosen_c=cval; chosen_gain=...
        if chosen is None: break
        h=snap
        h[i]+=chosen[2]; h[j]-=chosen[2]
        cur_c=chosen_c
        if cur_c < best_c - 1e-14: best_c=cur_c; best_h=h.copy()
        rounds +=1
    # if best_h differs maybe return best_h
    return best_h,best_c
```

Now, SLSQP LSE. In `slsqp_lse`, objective and grad stable. Need top-level:
```
def obj_lse(x,n,beta,deadline):
   if deadline and time.time()>deadline: raise RuntimeError
   c=c5_vec(x,n)
   ma=float(c.max())
   z=beta*(c-ma)
   # sum exp
   zmax=float(z.max())
   s=np.sum(np.exp(z-zmax))
   val=ma + (zmax + np.log(s))/beta
   return float(val)
def grad_lse(x,n,beta,deadline):
   if deadline... 
   c=c5_vec(x,n); ma=c.max(); z=beta*(c-ma); zmax=z.max(); w=np.exp(z-zmax); w/=w.sum(); return grad_weights(w,x,n)
```
For beta huge, zmax=0. If beta small, zmax maybe 0 because ma max. Good.

Could use `beta` in (100,1000,10000,100000,1000000). SLSQP maxiter maybe 150. If objective function time deadline, raise.

SLSQP equality constraint. Need top-level `cons_fun`, `cons_jac` as above.
```
def slsqp_lse(h,n,beta,deadline,maxit):
   y=project_feasible(h,n)
   bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
   cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':eq_con,'jac':eq_jac,'args':(n,)}]
   try:
      res=minimize(obj_lse,y,args=(n,beta,deadline),jac=grad_lse,method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,options={'maxiter':maxit,'ftol':1e-16})
      return project_feasible(res.x,n)
   except Exception:
      return None
```
But SLSQP may be sensitive to ftol; use 1e-14.

c1 = a*(1-b)+b*(1-c)

So index m corresponds? For m=0 (most negative lag?): c0 = a*(1-c) = h0*(1-h2), distance 2 left*(1-right). For m=4: c4 = c*(1-a) = h2*(1-h0), distance2 right*(1-left). For m=1: pairs distance1: h0(1-h1)+h1(1-h2) (left*(1-right) shifted). For m=3: distance1: h1(1-h0)+h2(1-h1) (right*(1-left)). This matches: m=mid-d = d? For d=1: m=1 (mid=2) is negative lag? It's left*(1-right). m=mid+d=3 is right*(1-left). Good. My earlier mapping wrong: For k negative (d), term h_i(1-h_{i+d}) (left*(1-right)); for k positive (d), term h_{i+d}(1-h_i) (right*(1-left)). Gradient formulas for pair distance d:
- weight at mid-d (call wm[d]): term left*(1-right): grad_left=1-right, grad_right=-left.
- weight at mid+d (wp[d]): term right*(1-left): grad_left=-right, grad_right=1-left.
Our combined formula:
grad_left += wm[d]*(1-h_right) + wp[d]*(-h_right) = wm[d] - (wm+wp)h_right.
grad_right += wm[d]*(-h_left) + wp[d]*(1-h_left) = wp[d] - (wm+wp)h_left.
Matches earlier. Good. For n=2 mid=1: m0=wm1: h0(1-h1), m2=wp1: h1(1-h0). Works.

Thus gradient mapping with `wp[1:] = w[mid+1:]`, `wm[1:] = w[:mid][::-1]` correct: d=1: wp[1]=w[mid+1] (right), wm[1]=w[mid-1] (left). d=n-1: wp[n-1]=w[2n-2], wm[n-1]=w[0]. Good.

Pseudo code:
```
def optimize_general(start_h, n, deadline, quality=2, seed=42):
    h = project_feasible(start_h, n)
    best_h = h.copy(); best_c = compute_c5(h,n)
    cur_h = h.copy(); cur_c = best_c
    if quality == 2:
        temps = (0.03, 0.01, 0.003, 0.001, 0.0003, 0.0001)
    elif quality == 1:
        temps = (0.01, 0.001, 0.0001)
    else:
        temps = (0.01, 0.001)
    maxit = 110 if n <= 350 else 55
    mus = (100, 1000)
    for temp in temps:
        if time.time() > deadline - 15: break
        c_cur = c5_vec(cur_h,n); cur_c = float(c_cur.max())
        if cur_c < best_c:
            best_c=cur_c; best_h=cur_h.copy()
        for mu in mus:
            if time.time() > deadline - 10: break
            if time.time() > deadline - 5: break
            w = active_weights(c_cur, temp)
            val_cur = float(w.dot(c_cur)) + mu*(float(cur_h.sum()) - 0.5*n)**2
            old_h = cur_h.copy()
            new_h = lbfgs_minimize(cur_h,n,w,mu,maxit,deadline)
            if new_h is None: continue
            c_new = c5_vec(new_h,n); cnew=float(c_new.max())
            val_new = float(w.dot(c_new)) + mu*(float(new_h.sum()) -0.5*n)**2
            if cnew < cur_c - 1e-12:
                cur_h=new_h; cur_c=cnew
            elif val_new < val_cur - 1e-9 and cnew < cur_c + 4e-6:
                cur_h=new_h; cur_c=cnew
            else:
                cur_h=old_h
            if cur_c < best_c:
                best_c=cur_c; best_h=cur_h.copy()
    # SLSQP
    if quality >=1:
        cnt=3 if quality==2 else 1
        for idx in range(cnt):
            if time.time() > deadline - 20: break
            beta = [500, 5000, 50000, 500000][idx] if quality>=2 else 2000
            old=best_h.copy()
            new=slsqp_lse(old,n,beta,deadline,80 if n>350 else 150)
            if new is None: continue
            cnew=compute_c5(new,n)
            if cnew < best_c - 1e-13:
                best_c=cnew; best_h=new
                cur_h=new.copy(); cur_c=cnew
            elif cnew < cur_c - 1e-12:
                cur_c=cnew; cur_h=new
    # pair polish from best_h
    if time.time() < deadline - 5:
        hpol = pair_polish(best_h, n, deadline - 2, seed=seed)
        if hpol[1] < best_c: best_h,hc = hpol...
    return best_h, best_c
```

def variant_noisy(h,n,seed,amp=0.05):
   rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
   x=np.linspace(0,2,n)
   h2=h.copy()
   for k in range(1,7):
      h2 += (amp/float(k+1))*rng.uniform(-1,1)*np.sin(np.pi*k*x/2 + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
   return project_feasible(h2,n)
```

Now, code line count. Let's draft final code in a text editor mentally.

```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def c5_vec(h,n):
    dx=2.0/n
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')*dx

def compute_c5(h,n):
    return float(np.max(c5_vec(h,n)))

def project_feasible(x,n):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float).ravel()
    if x.size != n:
        return x
    target=0.5*n
    if x.min() >= 0.0 and x.max() <= 1.0:
        diff=target-x.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            return x
        if diff > 0:
            room=1.0-x
            tot=room.sum()
            if tot > 1e-15:
                x=x+diff*room/tot
            else:
                return np.clip(x,0,1)
        else:
            tot=x.sum()
            if tot > 1e-15:
                x=x*(1.0+diff/tot)
            else:
                return np.zeros(n)
        return np.clip(x,0,1)
    lo=float(x.min())-1.0
    hi=float(x.max())
    # bisection
    for _ in range(70):
       mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
       if np.clip(x-mid,0.0,1.0).sum() > target:
          lo=mid
       else:
          hi=mid
    p=np.clip(x-0.5*(lo+hi),0.0,1.0)
    diff=target-p.sum()
    if diff > 1e-14:
       mask=p<1.0
       if mask.any(): p[mask]=np.clip(p[mask]+diff/mask.sum(),0,1)
    elif diff < -1e-14:
       mask=p>0.0
       if mask.any(): p[mask]=np.clip(p[mask]+diff/mask.sum(),0,1)
    return p
```
Note: if x in bounds but sum slightly off, projection proportional may make small out-of-bounds due floating? clip. Good.
If diff negative, `x*(1+diff/tot)`: Since sum tot > target? If x sum > target, diff negative, scale in (0,1). Good.
If x has all zeros and diff>0, tot room=n, adds uniform. Good.
But if x in bounds with sum < target but some room=0? phrase okay.

`active_weights`:
```
def active_weights(c, temp):
    c=np.asarray(c)
    mx=float(c.max())
    if temp <=0:
       mask = c >= mx - 1e-12
    else:
       mask = c >= mx - temp
    if not mask.any():
       mask[int(np.argmax(c))] = True
    w=np.zeros(c.shape, dtype=float)
    w[mask]=1.0
    w/=w.sum()
    return w
```
If temp very small, all true if c all equal. okay.

`grad_weights` as above. Need if `w` all zeros? no.
```
def grad_weights(w,h,n):
    g=np.zeros(n)
    mid=n-1
    w0=w[mid]
    if w0:
       g += w0*(1.0-2.0*h)
    wp=w[mid+1:]
    wm=w[:mid][::-1]
    nz=np.nonzero(wp != 0.0 or wm != 0.0)[0]
```
But `wp !=0.0 or wm` syntax invalid for arrays. Use:
```
    nz = np.nonzero((wp != 0.0) | (wm != 0.0))[0] + 1
    for d in nz:
       wpl=wp[d-1]; wml=wm[d-1]; a=wpl+wml
       left=n-d
       g[:left] += wml - a*h[d:]
       g[d:] += wpl - a*h[:left]
    return g*(2.0/n)
```
If `w` from softmax has tiny values, nonzero all. Fine. Could threshold `>1e-300`. Not needed.

`obj_weighted`:
```
def obj_weighted(x,n,w,mu,deadline=None):
    if deadline is not None and time.time() > deadline:
        raise RuntimeError
    c=c5_vec(x,n)
    val=float(np.dot(w,c))
    d=float(x.sum())-0.5*n
    if mu:
        val += mu*d*d
    return val
```
`grad_weighted` similarly. In grad, add `g += 2.0*mu*d*np.ones(n)`. Allocating ones; use `g.fill?` We can do `g = g + 2*mu*d` broadcasts scalar, no ones? In numpy, `g += 2.0*mu*d` works broadcasting scalar to array. Good.
```
def grad_weighted(x,n,w,mu,deadline=None):
    if deadline is not None and time.time() > deadline: raise RuntimeError
    g=grad_weights(w,x,n)
    d=float(x.sum())-0.5*n
    if mu:
        g += 2.0*mu*d
    return g
```

`lbfgs_minimize`:
```
def lbfgs_minimize(h,n,w,mu,maxiter,deadline):
    y=project_feasible(h,n)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    opts={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-16,'gtol':1e-12,'maxfun':max(200,20*maxiter),'maxls':40}
    try:
       res=minimize(obj_weighted,y,args=(n,w,mu,deadline),jac=grad_weighted,method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=bounds,options=opts)
       return project_feasible(res.x,n)
    except Exception:
       return None
```
Potential if deadline None? We always pass. Good. If maxiter small, maxfun min.

`obj_lse`, `grad_lse` top-level.
```
def obj_lse(x,n,beta,deadline=None):
    if deadline is not None and time.time()>deadline: raise RuntimeError
    c=c5_vec(x,n); ma=float(c.max()); z=beta*(c-ma); zm=float(z.max())
    s=np.sum(np.exp(z-zm))
    return ma + (zm + float(np.log(s)))/beta

def grad_lse(x,n,beta,deadline=None):
    if deadline ...
    c=...; ma=...; z=beta*(c-ma); zm=...
    w=np.exp(z-zm); w/=w.sum()
    return grad_weights(w,x,n)
```
For beta very large, `z` all <=0. If c has nan? no.

`eq_con`, `eq_jac`:
```
def eq_con(x,n): return float(x.sum()-0.5*n)
def eq_jac(x,n): return np.ones(x.shape[0])
```
`eq_jac` returns new ones. Could use `np.ones_like(x)`.

`slsqp_lse`:
```
def slsqp_lse(h,n,beta,deadline,maxiter):
    y=project_feasible(h,n)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':eq_con,'jac':eq_jac,'args':(n,)}]
    opts={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-15}
    try:
       res=minimize(obj_lse,y,args=(n,beta,deadline),jac=grad_lse,method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,options=opts)
       return project_feasible(res.x,n)
    except Exception:
       return None
```
Could SLSQP callback? no.

`pair_polish` code. Need no lambdas. Let's write carefully.
```
def pair_polish(h,n,deadline,seed=42):
    h=project_feasible(h,n).copy()
    cur_c=compute_c5(h,n)
    best_c=cur_c; best_h=h.copy()
    bases=(0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001,0.0005,0.0002,0.0001)
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    for base in bases:
        rounds=0
        max_rounds=200 if n<=350 else 80
        while time.time() < deadline and rounds < max_rounds:
            c=c5_vec(h,n); cm=float(c.max())
            if cm < cur_c - 1e-14:
                cur_c=cm
                if cm < best_c:
                    best_c=cm; best_h=h.copy()
            w=active_weights(c, 1e-4)
            g=grad_weights(w,h,n)
            order=np.argsort(g)
            K=min(60,n//3)
            lows=order[:K]
            highs=order[-K:][::-1]
            candidates=[]
            for t in range(K):
                candidates.append((lows[t], highs[t]))
            st=max(1,n//80)
            for i in range(0,n,st):
                for d in (1,2,3,5):
                    j=i+d
                    if j<n:
                        candidates.append((i,j))
            for _ in range(40):
                i=rng.randint(n)
                j=rng.randint(n)
                if i != j:
                    candidates.append((i,j))
            snap=h.copy()
            chosen=None
            chosen_c=cur_c
            for pair in candidates:
                i,j=pair
                if i == j:
                    continue
                diffg = g[i] - g[j]
                if diffg < 0.0:
                    delta = min(base, 1.0-snap[i], snap[j])
                else:
                    delta = -min(base, snap[i], 1.0-snap[j])
                if delta == 0.0:
                    continue
                snap[i] += delta
                snap[j] -= delta
                cn = float(np.max(c5_vec(snap,n)))
                snap[i] -= delta
                snap[j] += delta
                if cn < chosen_c - 1e-14:
                    chosen=(i,j,delta)
                    chosen_c=cn
            if chosen is None:
                break
            i,j,delta = chosen
            h = snap
            h[i] += delta
            h[j] -= delta
            cur_c = chosen_c
            if cur_c < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_c = cur_c
                best_h = h.copy()
            rounds += 1
    # ensure final best
    if compute_c5(h,n) < best_c:
        best_c=compute_c5(h,n); best_h=h.copy()
    return best_h, best_c
```

Now, `optimize_general`.
```
def optimize_general(start_h,n,deadline,quality=2,seed=42):
    h=project_feasible(start_h,n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
    cur_h=h.copy(); cur_c=best_c
    if quality==2:
       temps=(0.03,0.01,0.003,0.001,0.0003,0.0001)
    elif quality==1:
       temps=(0.01,0.001,0.0001)
    else:
       temps=(0.02,0.002)
    maxit=110 if n<=350 else 50
    if quality<2: maxit=60
    mus=(100,1000)
    for temp in temps:
       if time.time() > deadline - 15: break
       for mu in mus:
          if time.time() > deadline - 10: break
          c_cur=c5_vec(cur_h,n); cur_c=float(c_cur.max())
          if cur_c < best_c:
             best_c=cur_c; best_h=cur_h.copy()
          w=active_weights(c_cur,temp)
          val_cur=float(w.dot(c_cur)) + mu*(float(cur_h.sum())-0.5*n)**2
          old_h=cur_h.copy()
          new_h=lbfgs_minimize(cur_h,n,w,mu,maxit,deadline-5)
          if new_h is None:
             cur_h=old_h
             continue
          c_new=c5_vec(new_h,n); cn=float(c_new.max())
          val_new=float(w.dot(c_new)) + mu*(float(new_h.sum())-0.5*n)**2
          if cn < cur_c - 1e-12 or (val_new < val_cur - 1e-9 and cn < cur_c + 4e-6):
              cur_h=new_h; cur_c=cn
          else:
              cur_h=old_h
          if cur_c < best_c:
              best_c=cur_c; best_h=cur_h.copy()
    # SLSQP
    if quality>=1:
       if quality==2:
          sl_betas=(500,5000,50000,500000)
       else:
          sl_betas=(2000,20000)
       for beta in sl_betas:
          if time.time() > deadline - 20: break
          old=best_h.copy()
          new=slsqp_lse(old,n,beta,deadline-10,80 if n>350 else 120)
          if new is None: continue
          cn=compute_c5(new,n)
          if cn < best_c - 1e-13:
              best_c=cn; best_h=new
              cur_h=new.copy(); cur_c=cn
          elif cn < cur_c - 1e-12:
              cur_h=new; cur_c=cn
    if time.time() < deadline - 5:
       hp=pair_polish(best_h,n,deadline-2,seed=seed)
       if hp[1] < best_c:
          best_h,best_c=hp
    return best_h,best_c
```

```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    deadline=t0+budget_s-5
    try:
       h0=np.asarray(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy()
       n0=int(len(h0))
    except Exception:
       n0=300
       h0=np.full(n0,0.5)
    if n0 < 10:
       h0=np.full(300,0.5); n0=300
    h0=project_feasible(h0,n0)
    best_h=h0; best_c=compute_c5(h0,n0); bn=n0
    # main
    if deadline - t0 > 60:
       main_end = min(deadline - 180, t0 + 520)
       if main_end < t0 + 60:
          main_end = deadline - 20
       hh,cc=optimize_general(best_h,bn,main_end,quality=2,seed=seed)
       if cc < best_c: best_h, best_c, bn = hh, cc, len(hh)
    # sym variant if time
    if time.time() < deadline - 160 and bn < 500:
       hs=variant_sym(best_h,bn)
       t_end=min(deadline-100, time.time()+130)
       hh,cc=optimize_general(hs,bn,t_end,quality=1,seed=seed+1)
       if cc<best_c: update
    # noisy
    if time.time() < deadline - 120:
       hn=variant_noisy(best_h,bn,seed+2,0.03)
       t_end=min(deadline-80, time.time()+100)
       hh,cc=optimize_general(hn,bn,t_end,quality=1,seed=seed+3)
       update
    # threshold
    if time.time() < deadline - 90:
       ht=variant_threshold(best_h,bn,seed+4)
       t_end=min(deadline-60,time.time()+70)
       hh,cc=optimize_general(ht,bn,t_end,quality=0,seed=seed+5)
       update
    # coarse
    if time.time() < deadline - 150:
       nc=max(60,bn//3)
       hc=interp_h(best_h,bn,nc)
       t_c=min(deadline-80, time.time()+90)
       hc,cc=optimize_general(hc,nc,t_c,quality=1,seed=seed+6)
       h_up=interp_h(hc,nc,bn)
       t_up=min(deadline-40, time.time()+60)
       h_up,c_up=optimize_general(h_up,bn,t_up,quality=0,seed=seed+7)
       if c_up<best_c: update
    # higher n
    if time.time() < deadline - 120:
       n2 = 400
       if bn >= 400:
           n2 = min(600, bn + 100)
       if n2 <= 1000 and n2 != bn:
           h2=interp_h(best_h,bn,n2)
           t2=min(deadline-30, time.time()+160)
           h2,c2=optimize_general(h2,n2,t2,quality=1,seed=seed+8)
           if c2<best_c: update
    # final polish
    if time.time() < deadline - 3:
       hfin,cfin=pair_polish(best_h,len(best_h),deadline-1,seed=seed+10)
       if cfin<best_c: update
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,len(best_h))
    best_c=compute_c5(best_h,len(best_h))
    return best_h,best_c,len(best_h)
```

`variant_noisy`:
```
def variant_noisy(h,n,seed,amp):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    x=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n)
    y=h.copy()
    for k in range(1,6):
       y += (amp/float(k+1))*rng.uniform(-1.0,1.0)*np.sin(np.pi*float(k)*x/2.0 + rng.uniform(0.0,2.0*np.pi))
    return project_feasible(y,n)
```
`variant_threshold`:
```
def variant_threshold(h,n,seed):
   rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
   th=0.5+rng.uniform(-0.05,0.05)
   return project_feasible((h>th).astype(float),n)
```

```
def variant_sym(h,n):
   y=0.5*(h+h[::-1])
   return project_feasible(y,n)

```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def c5_vec(h,n):
    dx=2.0/n
    return np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')*dx

def compute_c5(h,n):
    return float(np.max(c5_vec(h,n)))

def project_feasible(x,n):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float).ravel()
    if x.size != n:
        return x
    target=0.5*n
    if x.min() >= 0.0 and x.max() <= 1.0:
        diff=target-x.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            return x
        if diff > 0:
            room=1.0-x
            tot=room.sum()
            if tot > 1e-15:
                x=x+diff*room/tot
            else:
                return np.clip(x,0.0,1.0)
        else:
            tot=x.sum()
            if tot > 1e-15:
                x=x*(1.0+diff/tot)
            else:
                return np.zeros(n)
        return np.clip(x,0.0,1.0)
    lo=float(x.min())-1.0
    hi=float(x.max())
    for _ in range(70):
        mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
        if np.clip(x-mid,0.0,1.0).sum() > target:
            lo=mid
        else:
            hi=mid
    p=np.clip(x-0.5*(lo+hi),0.0,1.0)
    diff=target-p.sum()
    if diff > 1e-13:
        m=p<1.0
        if m.any():
            p[m]=np.clip(p[m]+diff/float(m.sum()),0.0,1.0)
    elif diff < -1e-13:
        m=p>0.0
        if m.any():
            p[m]=np.clip(p[m]+diff/float(m.sum()),0.0,1.0)
    return p

def active_weights(c,temp):
    mx=float(np.max(c))
    if temp <= 0.0:
        mask=c >= mx-1e-12
    else:
        mask=c >= mx-temp
    if not mask.any():
        mask[int(np.argmax(c))]=True
    w=np.zeros(c.shape,dtype=float)
    w[mask]=1.0
    w/=w.sum()
    return w

def grad_weights(w,h,n):
    g=np.zeros(n,dtype=float)
    mid=n-1
    w0=w[mid]
    if w0 != 0.0:
        g += w0*(1.0-2.0*h)
    wp=w[mid+1:]
    wm=w[:mid][::-1]
    nz=np.nonzero((wp != 0.0) | (wm != 0.0))[0] + 1
    for d in nz:
        wpl=wp[d-1]
        wml=wm[d-1]
        a=wpl+wml
        if a == 0.0:
            continue
        left=n-d
        g[:left] += wml - a*h[d:]
        g[d:] += wpl - a*h[:left]
    return g*(2.0/n)

def obj_weighted(x,n,w,mu,deadline=None):
    if deadline is not None and time.time() > deadline:
        raise RuntimeError
    c=c5_vec(x,n)
    val=float(np.dot(w,c))
    if mu:
        s=float(x.sum())-0.5*n
        val += mu*s*s
    return val

def grad_weighted(x,n,w,mu,deadline=None):
    if deadline is not None and time.time() > deadline:
        raise RuntimeError
    g=grad_weights(w,x,n)
    if mu:
        s=float(x.sum())-0.5*n
        g += 2.0*mu*s
    return g

def lbfgs_minimize(h,n,w,mu,maxiter,deadline):
    y=project_feasible(h,n)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    opts={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-16,'gtol':1e-12,'maxfun':max(200,20*maxiter),'maxls':40}
    try:
        res=minimize(obj_weighted,y,args=(n,w,mu,deadline),jac=grad_weighted,method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=bounds,options=opts)
        return project_feasible(res.x,n)
    except Exception:
        return None

def obj_lse(x,n,beta,deadline=None):
    if deadline is not None and time.time() > deadline:
        raise RuntimeError
    c=c5_vec(x,n)
    ma=float(np.max(c))
    z=beta*(c-ma)
    zm=float(np.max(z))
    s=np.sum(np.exp(z-zm))
    return ma + (zm + float(np.log(s)))/beta

def grad_lse(x,n,beta,deadline=None):
    if deadline is not None and time.time() > deadline:
        raise RuntimeError
    c=c5_vec(x,n)
    ma=float(np.max(c))
    z=beta*(c-ma)
    zm=float(np.max(z))
    w=np.exp(z-zm)
    w/=w.sum()
    return grad_weights(w,x,n)

def eq_con(x,n):
    return float(x.sum()-0.5*n)

def eq_jac(x,n):
    return np.ones(x.shape[0],dtype=float)

def slsqp_lse(h,n,beta,deadline,maxiter):
    y=project_feasible(h,n)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':eq_con,'jac':eq_jac,'args':(n,)}]
    opts={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-15}
    try:
        res=minimize(obj_lse,y,args=(n,beta,deadline),jac=grad_lse,method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,options=opts)
        return project_feasible(res.x,n)
    except Exception:
        return None

def pair_polish(h,n,deadline,seed=42):
    h=project_feasible(h,n).copy()
    cur_c=compute_c5(h,n)
    best_c=cur_c
    best_h=h.copy()
    bases=(0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001,0.0005,0.0002,0.0001)
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    max_rounds=220 if n <= 350 else 80
    for base in bases:
        rounds=0
        while time.time() < deadline and rounds < max_rounds:
            c=c5_vec(h,n)
            cm=float(np.max(c))
            if cm < cur_c - 1e-14:
                cur_c=cm
                if cm < best_c:
                    best_c=cm; best_h=h.copy()
            w=active_weights(c,1e-4)
            g=grad_weights(w,h,n)
            order=np.argsort(g)
            K=min(60,n//3)
            lows=order[:K]
            highs=order[-K:][::-1]
            candidates=[]
            for t in range(K):
                candidates.append((lows[t], highs[t]))
            st=max(1,n//80)
            for i in range(0,n,st):
                for d in (1,2,3,5):
                    j=i+d
                    if j<n:
                        candidates.append((i,j))
            for _ in range(40):
                i=rng.randint(n)
                j=rng.randint(n)
                if i != j:
                    candidates.append((i,j))
            snap=h.copy()
            chosen=None
            chosen_c=cur_c
            for pair in candidates:
                i,j=pair
                if i == j:
                    continue
                diffg=g[i]-g[j]
                if diffg < 0.0:
                    delta=min(base,1.0-snap[i],snap[j])
                else:
                    delta=-min(base,snap[i],1.0-snap[j])
                if delta == 0.0:
                    continue
                snap[i]+=delta
                snap[j]-=delta
                cn=float(np.max(c5_vec(snap,n)))
                snap[i]-=delta
                snap[j]+=delta
                if cn < chosen_c - 1e-14:
                    chosen=(i,j,delta)
                    chosen_c=cn
            if chosen is None:
                break
            i,j,delta=chosen
            h=snap
            h[i]+=delta
            h[j]-=delta
            cur_c=chosen_c
            if cur_c < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_c=cur_c
                best_h=h.copy()
            rounds += 1
    cn=compute_c5(h,n)
    if cn < best_c:
        best_c=cn; best_h=h.copy()
    return best_h,best_c

def optimize_general(start_h,n,deadline,quality=2,seed=42):
    h=project_feasible(start_h,n)
    best_h=h.copy()
    best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
    cur_h=h.copy()
    cur_c=best_c
    if quality == 2:
        temps=(0.03,0.01,0.003,0.001,0.0003,0.0001)
        maxit=100
    elif quality == 1:
        temps=(0.01,0.001,0.0001)
        maxit=70
    else:
        temps=(0.02,0.002)
        maxit=45
    if n > 350:
        maxit=35
    mus=(100,1000)
    for temp in temps:
        if time.time() > deadline - 15:
            break
        for mu in mus:
            if time.time() > deadline - 10:
                break
            c_cur=c5_vec(cur_h,n)
            cur_c=float(np.max(c_cur))
            if cur_c < best_c:
                best_c=cur_c; best_h=cur_h.copy()
            w=active_weights(c_cur,temp)
            s_cur=float(cur_h.sum())-0.5*n
            val_cur=float(np.dot(w,c_cur)) + mu*s_cur*s_cur
            old_h=cur_h.copy()
            new_h=lbfgs_minimize(cur_h,n,w,mu,maxit,deadline)
            if new_h is None:
                cur_h=old_h
                continue
            c_new=c5_vec(new_h,n)
            cn=float(np.max(c_new))
            s_new=float(new_h.sum())-0.5*n
            val_new=float(np.dot(w,c_new)) + mu*s_new*s_new
            if cn < cur_c - 1e-12 or (val_new < val_cur - 1e-9 and cn < cur_c + 4e-6):
                cur_h=new_h
                cur_c=cn
            else:
                cur_h=old_h
            if cur_c < best_c:
                best_c=cur_c
                best_h=cur_h.copy()
    if quality >= 1:
        if quality == 2:
            sl_betas=(500,5000,50000,500000)
        else:
            sl_betas=(2000,20000)
        for beta in sl_betas:
            if time.time() > deadline - 20:
                break
            new=slsqp_lse(best_h,n,beta,deadline,80 if n > 350 else 110)
            if new is None:
                continue
            cn=compute_c5(new,n)
            if cn < best_c - 1e-13:
                best_c=cn
                best_h=new
                cur_h=new.copy()
                cur_c=cn
            elif cn < cur_c - 1e-12:
                cur_h=new
                cur_c=cn
    if time.time() < deadline - 5:
        hp=pair_polish(best_h,n,deadline-2,seed)
        if hp[1] < best_c:
            best_h,best_c=hp
    return best_h,best_c

def interp_h(h,n0,n1):
    x0=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n0)
    x1=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n1)
    return project_feasible(np.interp(x1,x0,h),n1)

def variant_sym(h,n):
    y=0.5*(h+h[::-1])
    return project_feasible(y,n)

def variant_noisy(h,n,seed,amp):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    x=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n)
    y=h.copy()
    for k in range(1,6):
        y += (amp/float(k+1))*rng.uniform(-1.0,1.0)*np.sin(np.pi*float(k)*x/2.0 + rng.uniform(0.0,2.0*np.pi))
    return project_feasible(y,n)

def variant_threshold(h,n,seed):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    th=0.5 + rng.uniform(-0.05,0.05)
    return project_feasible((h > th).astype(float),n)

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    deadline=t0+budget_s-5
    try:
        h0=np.asarray(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy()
        n0=int(len(h0))
    except Exception:
        n0=300
        h0=np.full(n0,0.5,dtype=float)
    if n0 < 20:
        n0=300
        h0=np.full(n0,0.5,dtype=float)
    h0=project_feasible(h0,n0)
    best_h=h0
    best_c=compute_c5(h0,n0)
    bn=n0
    if deadline - t0 > 80:
        main_end=min(deadline-150,t0+650)
        if main_end < t0+60:
            main_end=deadline-20
        hh,cc=optimize_general(best_h,bn,main_end,quality=2,seed=seed)
        if cc < best_c:
            best_h,best_c=hh,cc
            bn=len(best_h)
    if time.time() < deadline - 150:
        hs=variant_sym(best_h,bn)
        t_end=min(deadline-110,time.time()+120)
        hh,cc=optimize_general(hs,bn,t_end,quality=1,seed=seed+1)
        if cc < best_c:
            best_h,best_c=hh,cc
            bn=len(best_h)
    if time.time() < deadline - 120:
        hn=variant_noisy(best_h,bn,seed+2,0.03)
        t_end=min(deadline-90,time.time()+90)
        hh,cc=optimize_general(hn,bn,t_end,quality=1,seed=seed+3)
        if cc < best_c:
            best_h,best_c=hh,cc
            bn=len(best_h)
    if time.time() < deadline - 100:
        ht=variant_threshold(best_h,bn,seed+4)
        t_end=min(deadline-70,time.time()+60)
        hh,cc=optimize_general(ht,bn,t_end,quality=0,seed=seed+5)
        if cc < best_c:
            best_h,best_c=hh,cc
            bn=len(best_h)
    if time.time() < deadline - 180:
        nc=max(60,bn//3)
        hc=interp_h(best_h,bn,nc)
        t_c=min(deadline-90,time.time()+90)
        hc,cc=optimize_general(hc,nc,t_c,quality=1,seed=seed+6)
        h_up=interp_h(hc,nc,bn)
        t_up=min(deadline-40,time.time()+60)
        h_up,c_up=optimize_general(h_up,bn,t_up,quality=0,seed=seed+7)
        if c_up < best_c:
            best_h,best_c=h_up,c_up
            bn=len(best_h)
    if time.time() < deadline - 150:
        n2=400
        if bn >= 400:
            n2=min(600,bn+100)
        if n2 != bn and n2 < 1000:
            h2=interp_h(best_h,bn,n2)
            t2=min(deadline-35,time.time()+160)
            h2,c2=optimize_general(h2,n2,t2,quality=1,seed=seed+8)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_h,best_c=h2,c2
                bn=len(best_h)
    if time.time() < deadline - 2:
        hp=pair_polish(best_h,bn,deadline-1,seed+10)
        if hp[1] < best_c:
            best_h,best_c=hp
            bn=len(best_h)
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,bn)
    best_c=compute_c5(best_h,bn)
    return best_h,float(best_c),int(bn)
```

Let's think if a known construction maybe can be built explicitly and better. Could there be a simple h = 0.5 + A cos(πx) with A near 0.5 gives C=0.375? Let's approximate to see if our optimization might not need initial. For A=0.5, h=1/2+1/2cosπx. Let's compute continuous c(k) for numpy? For k small, c+? Use integral. For k positive (right*(1-left)): ∫0^{2-k} 0.25[1+cosπ(x+k)-cosπx - cosπ(x+k)cosπx]. Let's use identities. For k variable, maybe max at k=1? Let's derive quickly:
I1=∫0^L 1 dx = L.
I2=∫ cosπ(x+k) dx = [sinπ(x+k)/π]_0^L = (sinπ(L+k)-sinπk)/π.
I3=∫ cosπx dx = (sinπL -0)/π.
I4=0.5∫[cos(π(2x+k)) + cos(πk)] from 0 to L = 0.5[sin(π(2L+k))/(2π) - sin(πk)/(2π)? Wait ∫cos(2πx+πk)= sin(...)/(2π). plus 0.5 L cosπk. So I4 = 0.5[ (sin(2πL+πk)-sinπk)/(2π) + L cosπk].

def fourier_eval(p,n,modes):
    h=0.5*np.ones(n)
    for idx in range(len(modes)):
        h += p[idx]*modes[idx]
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    return compute_c5(h,n)

def fourier_search(n,deadline,seed):
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*(2.0/n)
    modes=[]
    for m in range(1,5):
        modes.append(np.cos(np.pi*m*x))
    modes=tuple(modes)
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    best_p=np.array([0.5,0.0,0.0,0.0])
    best_v=compute from best_p
    # coarse grid 3 modes: mode1 0..0.5 step0.05, mode2 -0.2..0.2 step0.05, mode3 -0.15..0.15 step0.05
    a_vals=np.arange(0.0,0.5+1e-12,0.05)
    b_vals=np.arange(-0.2,0.2+1e-12,0.05)
    c_vals=np.arange(-0.15,0.15+1e-12,0.05)
    for ai in a_vals:
      for bi in b_vals:
        for ci in c_vals:
          if time.time()>deadline-15: break...
          p=np.array([ai,bi,ci,0.0])
          v=fourier_eval(p,n,modes)
          update
    # random 500
    for _ in range(800):
      p=np.array([rng.uniform(0,0.5), rng.uniform(-0.3,0.3), rng.uniform(-0.2,0.2), rng.uniform(-0.15,0.15)])
      v=fourier_eval...
    # Nelder top starts? Use best and 3 random around best
    starts=[best_p]
    for _ in range(3):
       p=best_p + rng.uniform(-0.08,0.08,size=4)
       starts.append(p)
    for p0 in starts:
       if time.time()>deadline-5: break
       try:
          res=minimize(fourier_eval, p0, args=(n,modes), method='Nelder-Mead', options={'maxiter':800,'xatol':1e-6,'fatol':1e-9,'adaptive':True})
          p=np.asarray(res.x)
          v=fourier_eval(p,n,modes)
          if v<best_v: update
       except: pass
    h=project_feasible(0.5+sum(...),n)? Need return h.
    h=0.5*ones; for m: h += best_p[m]*modes[m]; h=project
    return h,best_v

Project compact:
```
def project_feasible(x,n):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float).ravel()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): x=np.where(np.isfinite(x),x,0.5)
    target=0.5*n
    if x.size==n and 0<=x.min()<=x.max()<=1:
        d=target-x.sum()
        if abs(d)<1e-12: return x
        if d>0:
            r=1-x
            return np.clip(x+d*r/r.sum() if r.sum()>0 else x,0,1)
        return np.clip(x*(1+d/x.sum() if x.sum()>0 else 0),0,1)
    lo,hi=x.min()-1,x.max()
    for _ in range(60):
        m=0.5*(lo+hi)
        if np.clip(x-m,0,1).sum()>target: lo=m
        else: hi=m
    p=np.clip(x-0.5*(lo+hi),0,1)
    d=target-p.sum()
    m=(p<1) if d>0 else p>0
    if m.any(): p[m]=np.clip(p[m]+d/m.sum(),0,1)
    return p
```
Need if x out of bounds but size? If x size !=n? Could happen? We'll assume. If x.size != n but within maybe. Use if x.size !=n: return x? Add line. Compact.

Potential bug: condition `0<=x.min()<=x.max()<=1` with x empty no. Fine.

`active_weights` compact:
```
def active_weights(c,t):
    mx=c.max()
    mask=c >= mx - (1e-12 if t<=0 else t)
    if not mask.any(): mask[int(c.argmax())]=1
    w=mask.astype(float); return w/w.sum()
```
`c.argmax()` returns int already? For 1D numpy argmax returns int, can do. If c is ndarray, `int(c.argmax())`.

`grad_weights` compact. Need no long loops? Keep.
```
def grad_weights(w,h,n):
    g=np.zeros(n); mid=n-1
    if w[mid]: g+=w[mid]*(1-2*h)
    wp=w[mid+1:]; wm=w[:mid][::-1]
    for d in np.nonzero((wp!=0)|(wm!=0))[0]+1:
        a=wp[d-1]+wm[d-1]; l=n-d
        if a:
            g[:l]+=wm[d-1]-a*h[d:]
            g[d:]+=wp[d-1]-a*h[:l]
    return g*2/n
```
Line long but okay.

`obj_weighted` and `grad_weighted`:
```
def obj_weighted(x,n,w,mu,deadline=None):
    if deadline and time.time()>deadline: raise RuntimeError
    c=c5_vec(x,n); s=x.sum()-0.5*n
    return float(w.dot(c))+mu*s*s if mu else float(w.dot(c))
```
Can't use if expression with `mu`? `mu` maybe 0/float. If mu=0, condition `if mu` works but if mu=0.0. Use `v=float(w.dot(c)); if mu: v+=mu*s*s; return v` two lines.

`grad`:
```
def grad_weighted(x,n,w,mu,deadline=None):
    if deadline and time.time()>deadline: raise RuntimeError
    g=grad_weights(w,x,n); s=x.sum()-0.5*n
    if mu: g+=2*mu*s
    return g
```

`lbfgs`:
```
def lbfgs_minimize(h,n,w,mu,it,dl):
    y=project_feasible(h,n); b=[(0,1)]*n
    try:
        r=minimize(obj_weighted,y,args=(n,w,mu,dl),jac=grad_weighted,method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=b,options={'maxiter':it,'ftol':1e-16,'gtol':1e-12,'maxfun':max(200,20*it),'maxls':40})
        return project_feasible(r.x,n)
    except Exception: return None
```

`obj_lse/grad_lse` compact.
```
def obj_lse(x,n,beta,dl=None):
    if dl and time.time()>dl: raise RuntimeError
    c=c5_vec(x,n); ma=c.max(); z=beta*(c-ma); zm=z.max()
    return float(ma+(zm+np.log(np.exp(z-zm).sum()))/beta)
def grad_lse(x,n,beta,dl=None):
    if dl and time.time()>dl: raise RuntimeError
    c=c5_vec(x,n); ma=c.max(); z=beta*(c-ma); zm=z.max()
    w=np.exp(z-zm); w/=w.sum(); return grad_weights(w,x,n)
```
`np.log(...).sum` okay.

`eq`:
```
def eq_con(x,n): return float(x.sum()-0.5*n)
def eq_jac(x,n): return np.ones_like(x)
```
`slsqp`:
```
def slsqp_lse(h,n,beta,dl,it):
    y=project_feasible(h,n); b=[(0,1)]*n; c=[{'type':'eq','fun':eq_con,'jac':eq_jac,'args':(n,)}]
    try:
        r=minimize(obj_lse,y,args=(n,beta,dl),jac=grad_lse,method='SLSQP',bounds=b,constraints=c,options={'maxiter':it,'ftol':1e-15})
        return project_feasible(r.x,n)
    except Exception: return None
```

`pair_polish` compact. Need reduce lines.
```
def pair_polish(h,n,dl,seed=42):
    h=project_feasible(h,n).copy(); bc=cc=compute_c5(h,n); bh=h.copy(); rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    for base in (0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001,0.0005,0.0002,0.0001):
        for _ in range(160 if n<=350 else 60):
            if time.time()>dl: return bh,bc
            c=c5_vec(h,n); cm=c.max()
            if cm<cc: cc=cm; bh=h.copy(); bc=cc
            w=active_weights(c,1e-4); g=grad_weights(w,h,n); ord=np.argsort(g); K=min(50,n//3)
            cand=list(zip(ord[:K],ord[-K:][::-1]))
            st=max(1,n//80)
            for i in range(0,n,st):
                for d in (1,2,4):
                    if i+d<n: cand.append((i,i+d))
            for _ in range(30):
                i,j=rng.randint(n),rng.randint(n)
                if i!=j: cand.append((i,j))
            sn=h.copy(); ch=None; chc=cc
            for i,j in cand:
                dg=g[i]-g[j]
                if dg<0: de=min(base,1-sn[i],sn[j])
                else: de=-min(base,sn[i],1-sn[j])
                if de==0: continue
                sn[i]+=de; sn[j]-=de
                cn=c5_vec(sn,n).max()
                sn[i]-=de; sn[j]+=de
                if cn<chc-1e-14:
                    ch=(i,j,de); chc=float(cn)
            if ch is None: break
            i,j,de=ch; h=sn; h[i]+=de; h[j]-=de; cc=chc
            if cc<bc: bc=cc; bh=h.copy()
    return bh,bc
```
This returns best_h/best_c. Note if no improvement at a base, break base and continue next. Good. We lost base-specific max rounds; `for _` loops. The `if ch is None: break` breaks inner for, outer base continues. Good.

`pair_polish` uses int(c.max())? cn float. Good.

Candidate dedup not. Good.

`optimize_general` compact:
```
def optimize_general(sh,n,dl,q=2,seed=42):
    h=project_feasible(sh,n); bh=h.copy(); bc=cc=compute_c5(h,n); ch=h.copy()
    temps=(0.03,0.01,0.003,0.001,0.0003,0.0001) if q==2 else ((0.01,0.001,0.0001) if q==1 else (0.02,0.002))
    mi=140 if q==2 else (80 if q==1 else 45)

            if nh is not None:

                if cn<bc: bc=cn; bh=nh; ch=nh.copy(); cc=cn

    if time.time()<dl-5:

        if pc<bc: bh,pc=...
    return bh,bc

Let's assemble final code:

```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def c5_vec(h, n):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full') * (2.0 / n)

def compute_c5(h, n):
    return float(np.max(c5_vec(h, n)))

def project_feasible(x, n):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float).ravel()
    if x.size != n:
        return x
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.5)
    target = 0.5 * n
    if x.min() >= 0.0 and x.max() <= 1.0:
        d = target - float(x.sum())
        if abs(d) < 1e-12:
            return x
        if d > 0:
            r = 1.0 - x
            s = float(r.sum())
            return np.clip(x + (d * r / s if s > 0 else 0.0), 0.0, 1.0)
        s = float(x.sum())
        return np.clip(x * (1.0 + d / s) if s > 0 else np.zeros(n), 0.0, 1.0)
    lo, hi = float(x.min()) - 1.0, float(x.max())
    for _ in range(60):
        m = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        if float(np.clip(x - m, 0.0, 1.0).sum()) > target:
            lo = m
        else:
            hi = m
    p = np.clip(x - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    d = target - float(p.sum())
    if d > 1e-12:
        m = p < 1.0
        if m.any():
            p[m] = np.clip(p[m] + d / float(m.sum()), 0.0, 1.0)
    elif d < -1e-12:
        m = p > 0.0
        if m.any():
            p[m] = np.clip(p[m] + d / float(m.sum()), 0.0, 1.0)
    return p
```
Line `return np.clip(x + (d * r / s if s > 0 else 0.0), 0.0, 1.0)` ternary with array add scalar okay. If s=0, x +0. Good. For diff<0 and s=0, returns zeros. Good.

`active_weights`:
```
def active_weights(c, t):
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    mask = c >= mx - (1e-12 if t <= 0 else t)
    if not bool(np.any(mask)):
        mask = np.zeros_like(c, dtype=bool)
        mask[int(np.argmax(c))] = True
    w = mask.astype(float)
    w /= w.sum()
    return w
```
If c already all? good. But if initial mask not all false, no need reassign. If false, we create. If c is numpy, `mask = np.zeros_like?` okay.

`grad_weights` as compact. Need if `w[mid]` is np.float. Use `if w[mid] != 0.0`. If w all zeros? no.

`obj_weighted`:
```
def obj_weighted(x,n,w,mu,deadline=None):
    if deadline is not None and time.time() > deadline:
        raise RuntimeError
    c = c5_vec(x,n)
    v = float(np.dot(w,c))
    if mu != 0:
        s = float(x.sum()) - 0.5*n
        v += mu*s*s
    return v
```
`grad` similar.

`lbfgs_minimize`. Use `[(0.0,1.0)] * n` okay.

`fourier_h` and objective.
```
def fourier_h(p,n,modes):
    h = 0.5 * np.ones(n)
    for i in range(len(modes)):
        h += p[i] * modes[i]
    return project_feasible(h,n)

def fourier_obj(p,n,modes):
    return compute_c5(fourier_h(p,n,modes), n)
```

`fourier_search` compact:
```
def fourier_search(n, dl, seed):
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    modes = []
    for k in range(1, 5):
        modes.append(np.cos(np.pi * k * x))
    modes = tuple(modes)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    bp = np.array([0.5, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0])
    bh = fourier_h(bp,n,modes)
    bv = compute_c5(bh,n)
    a_vals = np.arange(0.0, 0.51, 0.05)
    b_vals = np.arange(-0.20, 0.21, 0.05)
    c_vals = np.arange(-0.15, 0.16, 0.05)
    for a in a_vals:
        for b in b_vals:
            for c in c_vals:
                if time.time() > dl - 20:
                    return bh, bv
                p = np.array([a, b, c, 0.0])
                h = fourier_h(p,n,modes)
                v = compute_c5(h,n)
                if v < bv:
                    bv = v; bp = p.copy(); bh = h
    for _ in range(600):
        if time.time() > dl - 15:
            break
        p = np.array([rng.uniform(0.0,0.5), rng.uniform(-0.25,0.25), rng.uniform(-0.20,0.20), rng.uniform(-0.10,0.10)])
        h = fourier_h(p,n,modes)
        v = compute_c5(h,n)
        if v < bv:
            bv = v; bp = p.copy(); bh = h
    for _ in range(3):
        if time.time() > dl - 5:
            break
        p0 = bp + rng.uniform(-0.08,0.08,4)
        try:
            r = minimize(fourier_obj, p0, args=(n,modes), method='Nelder-Mead', options={'maxiter':250,'xatol':1e-6,'fatol':1e-9,'adaptive':True})
            h = fourier_h(r.x,n,modes); v = compute_c5(h,n)
            if v < bv:
                bv = v; bp = np.asarray(r.x).copy(); bh = h
        except Exception:
            pass
    return bh, float(bv)
```
Potential issue: first loop breaks? It returns inside grid to respect time. Good. If time exactly, returns. Random break. Nelder no deadline; but we check before each start. Could still overrun; maxiter 250 maybe okay.

Line `a_vals=np.arange(0.0,0.51,0.05)` may stop at 0.5 (includes 11 values). Good. `b_vals` -0.2 to 0.2 step 0.05? np.arange may include 0.199999 and not -0.2? -0.2 included? start -0.2, stop 0.21, step 0.05 -> -0.2,-0.15,...,0.2? yes maybe 9. `c_vals` 7.

`perturb_variant`:
```
def perturb_variant(h,n,seed,amp):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    y = h.copy()
    for k in range(1, 5):
        y += (amp / float(k + 1)) * rng.uniform(-1.0,1.0) * np.cos(np.pi*k*x + rng.uniform(0.0,6.283185307))
    return project_feasible(y,n)
```

`interp_h` as before.

Now, `optimize_general`. Need ensure all variables. Let's write concise:
```
def optimize_general(sh,n,dl,q=2,seed=42):
    h = project_feasible(sh,n)
    bh = h.copy(); bhv = compute_c5(h,n)
    ch = h.copy(); cc = bhv
    if q == 2: tempos = (0.03,0.01,0.003,0.001,0.0003,0.0001,0.0); mi=140
    elif q == 1: tempos=(0.01,0.001,0.0); mi=80
    else: tempos=(0.02,0.002); mi=45
    if n>350: mi=max(40, mi//2)
    for t in tempos:
        if time.time() > dl - 15: break
        for mu in (100,1000):
            if time.time() > dl - 8: break
            cw = c5_vec(ch,n); cc = float(np.max(cw))
            if cc < bhv: bhv=cc; bh=ch.copy()
            w = active_weights(cw,t)
            s = float(ch.sum()) - 0.5*n; ov = float(w.dot(cw)) + mu*s*s
            oh = ch.copy()
            nh = lbfgs_minimize(ch,n,w,mu,mi,dl)
            if nh is None:
                ch = oh; continue
            nw = c5_vec(nh,n); cn = float(np.max(nw))
            s2 = float(nh.sum()) - 0.5*n; nv = float(w.dot(nw)) + mu*s2*s2
            if cn < cc - 1e-12 or (nv < ov - 1e-9 and cn < cc + 4e-6):
                ch = nh; cc = cn
            else:
                ch = oh
            if cc < bhv: bhv=cc; bh=ch.copy()
    if q >= 1:
        bts=(500,5000,50000,500000) if q==2 else (2000,20000)
        for b in bts:
            if time.time() > dl - 20: break
            nh = slsqp_lse(bh,n,b,dl, 70 if n>350 else 110)
            if nh is not None:
                cn = compute_c5(nh,n)
                if cn < bhv:
                    bhv=cn; bh=nh; ch=nh.copy(); cc=cn
                elif cn < cc:
                    ch=nh; cc=cn
    if time.time() < dl - 5:
        ph,pc = pair_polish(bh,n,dl-2,seed)
        if pc < bhv: bh, bhv = ph, pc
    return bh, float(bhv)
```
Note variable name `tempos` typo? In Python, `tempos` is a variable, not keyword. Fine, but use `temps` for readability. We can use `temps` but line okay. Use `temps` to avoid confusion.

Potential issue: In `if q == 2: tempos = ...; mi=140` multiple statements on one line separated by semicolon okay. But line length maybe. Use compact with separate lines? Line count okay. We can write normal multi-line for readability but need <=300. Semicolons okay.

`slsqp_lse` maxit for n>350 70 else 110. Good.

Now, `run` compact:
```
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time(); dl=t0+budget_s-5
    try:
        h0=np.asarray(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy()
        n0=int(len(h0))
    except Exception:
        h0=np.full(300,0.5); n0=300
    if n0 < 20:
        h0=np.full(300,0.5); n0=300
    h0=project_feasible(h0,n0)
    bh=h0; bc=compute_c5(h0,n0)
    if dl - t0 > 220:
        fe = min(t0 + 70, dl - 220)
        if fe > t0 + 10:
            hf,cf = fourier_search(n0, fe, seed+11)
            if cf < bc: bh, bc = hf, cf
    me = min(dl - 150, t0 + 700)
    if me < t0 + 60: me = dl - 20
    hh, cc = optimize_general(bh, n0, me, 2, seed)
    if cc < bc: bh, bc, n0 = hh, cc, len(hh)
    if time.time() < dl - 130:
        hv = perturb_variant(bh, n0, seed+1, 0.03)
        hv, cc = optimize_general(hv, n0, min(dl - 90, time.time() + 80), 1, seed+2)
        if cc < bc: bh, bc = hv, cc
    if time.time() < dl - 160:
        n2 = 400 if n0 < 400 else min(600, n0 + 100)
        if n2 != n0 and n2 < 1000:
            h2 = interp_h(bh, n0, n2)
            hh, cc = optimize_general(h2, n2, min(dl - 35, time.time() + 150), 1, seed+3)
            if cc < bc: bh, bc = hh, cc
    if time.time() < dl - 2:
        ph, pc = pair_polish(bh, len(bh), dl - 1, seed+10)
        if pc < bc: bh, bc = ph, pc
    bh = project_feasible(bh, len(bh))
    bc = compute_c5(bh, len(bh))
    return bh, float(bc), int(len(bh))
```

Full code draft:

```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def c5_vec(h, n):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full') * (2.0 / n)

def compute_c5(h, n):
    return float(np.max(c5_vec(h, n)))

def project_feasible(x, n):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float).ravel()
    if x.size != n:
        return x
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.5)
    target = 0.5 * n
    if x.min() >= 0.0 and x.max() <= 1.0:
        d = target - float(x.sum())
        if abs(d) < 1e-12:
            return x
        if d > 0:
            r = 1.0 - x
            s = float(r.sum())
            return np.clip(x + (d * r / s if s > 0 else 0.0), 0.0, 1.0)
        s = float(x.sum())
        return np.clip(x * (1.0 + d / s) if s > 0 else np.zeros(n), 0.0, 1.0)
    lo, hi = float(x.min()) - 1.0, float(x.max())
    for _ in range(60):
        m = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        if float(np.clip(x - m, 0.0, 1.0).sum()) > target:
            lo = m
        else:
            hi = m
    p = np.clip(x - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    d = target - float(p.sum())
    if d > 1e-12:
        mask = p < 1.0
        if mask.any():
            p[mask] = np.clip(p[mask] + d / float(mask.sum()), 0.0, 1.0)
    elif d < -1e-12:
        mask = p > 0.0
        if mask.any():
            p[mask] = np.clip(p[mask] + d / float(mask.sum()), 0.0, 1.0)
    return p
```
If `s=0` in diff<0 branch, `x * (1.0 + d / s) if s > 0 else np.zeros(n)` works, but if s=0, d = target >0? Wait diff<0 means sum > target, so s>0. okay.

`active_weights`:
```
def active_weights(c, t):
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    mask = c >= mx - (1e-12 if t <= 0 else t)
    if not bool(np.any(mask)):
        mask = np.zeros(c.shape, dtype=bool)
        mask[int(np.argmax(c))] = True
    w = mask.astype(float)
    w /= w.sum()
    return w
```
Good.

`grad_weights`:
```
def grad_weights(w, h, n):
    g = np.zeros(n)
    mid = n - 1
    if w[mid] != 0.0:
        g += w[mid] * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
    wp = w[mid + 1:]
    wm = w[:mid][::-1]
    for d in np.nonzero((wp != 0.0) | (wm != 0.0))[0] + 1:
        wpl = wp[d - 1]
        wml = wm[d - 1]
        a = wpl + wml
        left = n - d
        if a != 0.0:
            g[:left] += wml - a * h[d:]
            g[d:] += wpl - a * h[:left]
    return g * (2.0 / n)
```
Good.

`obj_weighted`:
```
def obj_weighted(x, n, w, mu, deadline=None):
    if deadline is not None and time.time() > deadline:
        raise RuntimeError
    c = c5_vec(x, n)
    v = float(np.dot(w, c))
    if mu != 0:
        s = float(x.sum()) - 0.5 * n
        v += mu * s * s
    return v
```
`mu` maybe int/float. Good.

`grad_weighted` similar.

`lbfgs_minimize`:
```
def lbfgs_minimize(h, n, w, mu, it, dl):
    y = project_feasible(h, n)
    b = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    try:
        r = minimize(obj_weighted, y, args=(n, w, mu, dl), jac=grad_weighted, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=b, options={'maxiter': it, 'ftol': 1e-16, 'gtol': 1e-12, 'maxfun': max(200, 20 * it), 'maxls': 40})
        return project_feasible(r.x, n)
    except Exception:
        return None
```
Good.

`obj_lse`:
```
def obj_lse(x, n, beta, deadline=None):
    if deadline is not None and time.time() > deadline:
        raise RuntimeError
    c = c5_vec(x, n)
    ma = float(np.max(c))
    z = beta * (c - ma)
    zm = float(np.max(z))
    return float(ma + (zm + float(np.log(np.sum(np.exp(z - zm))))) / beta)
```
Good. `beta` nonzero.

`grad_lse` similar.

`eq`:
```
def eq_con(x, n):
    return float(x.sum() - 0.5 * n)

def eq_jac(x, n):
    return np.ones(x.shape[0], dtype=float)
```

`slsqp_lse`:
```
def slsqp_lse(h, n, beta, dl, it):
    y = project_feasible(h, n)
    b = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    cons = [{'type': 'eq', 'fun': eq_con, 'jac': eq_jac, 'args': (n,)}]
    try:
        r = minimize(obj_lse, y, args=(n, beta, dl), jac=grad_lse, method='SLSQP', bounds=b, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter': it, 'ftol': 1e-15})
        return project_feasible(r.x, n)
    except Exception:
        return None
```
Good.

`pair_polish`: Let's write exact syntax:
```
def pair_polish(h, n, dl, seed=42):
    h = project_feasible(h, n).copy()
    bc = cc = compute_c5(h, n)
    bh = h.copy()
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    bases = (0.02, 0.01, 0.005, 0.002, 0.001, 0.0005, 0.0002, 0.0001, 5e-5, 2e-5, 1e-5)
    for base in bases:
        for _ in range(160 if n <= 350 else 60):
            if time.time() > dl:
                return bh, bc
            c = c5_vec(h, n)
            cm = float(np.max(c))
            if cm < cc:
                cc = cm
                bh = h.copy()
                bc = cc
            w = active_weights(c, 1e-4)
            g = grad_weights(w, h, n)
            ord = np.argsort(g)
            K = min(50, max(1, n // 3))
            cand = list(zip(ord[:K], ord[-K:][::-1]))
            st = max(1, n // 80)
            for i in range(0, n, st):
                for d in (1, 2, 4):
                    if i + d < n:
                        cand.append((i, i + d))
            for _ in range(30):
                i = rng.randint(n)
                j = rng.randint(n)
                if i != j:
                    cand.append((i, j))
            sn = h.copy()
            ch = None
            chc = cc
            for i, j in cand:
                dg = g[i] - g[j]
                if dg < 0.0:
                    de = min(base, 1.0 - sn[i], sn[j])
                else:
                    de = -min(base, sn[i], 1.0 - sn[j])
                if de == 0.0:
                    continue
                sn[i] += de
                sn[j] -= de
                cn = float(np.max(c5_vec(sn, n)))
                sn[i] -= de
                sn[j] += de
                if cn < chc - 1e-14:
                    ch = (i, j, de)
                    chc = cn
            if ch is None:
                break
            i, j, de = ch
            h = sn
            h[i] += de
            h[j] -= de
            cc = chc
            if cc < bc:
                bc = cc
                bh = h.copy()
    return bh, bc
```
Good. If no improvement at base, break to next base. If improvement 160 rounds, continue next base. Good.

`optimize_general`:
```
def optimize_general(sh, n, dl, q=2, seed=42):
    h = project_feasible(sh, n)
    bh = h.copy()
    bhv = compute_c5(h, n)
    ch = h.copy()
    cc = bhv
    if q == 2:
        temps = (0.03, 0.01, 0.003, 0.001, 0.0003, 0.0001, 0.0)
        mi = 140
    elif q == 1:
        temps = (0.01, 0.001, 0.0)
        mi = 80
    else:
        temps = (0.02, 0.002)
        mi = 45
    if n > 350:
        mi = max(40, mi // 2)
    mus = (10, 100, 1000) if q == 2 else (100, 1000)
    for t in temps:
        if time.time() > dl - 15:
            break
        for mu in mus:
            if time.time() > dl - 8:
                break
            cw = c5_vec(ch, n)
            cc = float(np.max(cw))
            if cc < bhv:
                bhv = cc
                bh = ch.copy()
            w = active_weights(cw, t)
            s = float(ch.sum()) - 0.5 * n
            ov = float(w.dot(cw)) + mu * s * s
            oh = ch.copy()
            nh = lbfgs_minimize(ch, n, w, mu, mi, dl)
            if nh is None:
                ch = oh
                continue
            nw = c5_vec(nh, n)
            cn = float(np.max(nw))
            s2 = float(nh.sum()) - 0.5 * n
            nv = float(w.dot(nw)) + mu * s2 * s2
            if cn < cc - 1e-12 or (nv < ov - 1e-9 and cn < cc + 4e-6):
                ch = nh
                cc = cn
            else:
                ch = oh
            if cc < bhv:
                bhv = cc
                bh = ch.copy()
    if q >= 1:
        bts = (500, 5000, 50000, 500000) if q == 2 else (2000, 20000)
        for b in bts:
            if time.time() > dl - 20:
                break
            nh = slsqp_lse(bh, n, b, dl, 70 if n > 350 else 110)
            if nh is not None:
                cn = compute_c5(nh, n)
                if cn < bhv:
                    bhv = cn
                    bh = nh
                    ch = nh.copy()
                    cc = cn
                elif cn < cc:
                    ch = nh
                    cc = cn
    if time.time() < dl - 5:
        ph, pc = pair_polish(bh, n, dl - 2, seed)
        if pc < bhv:
            bh, bhv = ph, pc
    return bh, float(bhv)
```

def fourier_h(p, n, modes):
    h = 0.5 * np.ones(n)
    for i in range(len(modes)):
        h += p[i] * modes[i]
    return project_feasible(h, n)

def fourier_obj(p, n, modes):
    return compute_c5(fourier_h(p, n, modes), n)

```
def fourier_search(n, dl, seed):
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    modes = []
    for k in range(1, 5):
        modes.append(np.cos(np.pi * k * x))
    modes = tuple(modes)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    bp = np.array([0.5, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0])
    bh = fourier_h(bp, n, modes)
    bv = compute_c5(bh, n)
    a_vals = np.linspace(0.0, 0.5, 11)
    b_vals = np.linspace(-0.2, 0.2, 9)
    c_vals = np.linspace(-0.15, 0.15, 7)
    for a in a_vals:
        for b in b_vals:
            for c in c_vals:
                if time.time() > dl - 20:
                    return bh, float(bv)
                p = np.array([a, b, c, 0.0])
                h = fourier_h(p, n, modes)
                v = compute_c5(h, n)
                if v < bv:
                    bv = v
                    bp = p.copy()
                    bh = h
    for _ in range(600):
        if time.time() > dl - 15:
            break
        p = np.array([rng.uniform(0.0, 0.5), rng.uniform(-0.25, 0.25), rng.uniform(-0.2, 0.2), rng.uniform(-0.1, 0.1)])
        h = fourier_h(p, n, modes)
        v = compute_c5(h, n)
        if v < bv:
            bv = v
            bp = p.copy()
            bh = h
    for _ in range(3):
        if time.time() > dl - 5:
            break
        p0 = bp + rng.uniform(-0.08, 0.08, 4)
        try:
            r = minimize(fourier_obj, p0, args=(n, modes), method='Nelder-Mead', options={'maxiter': 250, 'xatol': 1e-6, 'fatol': 1e-9, 'adaptive': True})
            h = fourier_h(r.x, n, modes)
            v = compute_c5(h, n)
            if v < bv:
                bv = v
                bp = np.asarray(r.x).copy()
                bh = h
        except Exception:
            pass
    return bh, float(bv)
```

def perturb_variant(h, n, seed, amp):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    y = h.copy()
    for k in range(1, 5):
        y += (amp / float(k + 1)) * rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0) * np.cos(np.pi * k * x + rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
    return project_feasible(y, n)
```

def interp_h(h, n0, n1):
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n0)
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n1)
    return project_feasible(np.interp(x1, x0, h), n1)

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    dl = t0 + budget_s - 5
    try:
        h0 = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n0 = int(len(h0))
    except Exception:
        h0 = np.full(300, 0.5, dtype=float)
        n0 = 300
    if n0 < 20:
        h0 = np.full(300, 0.5, dtype=float)
        n0 = 300
    h0 = project_feasible(h0, n0)
    bh = h0
    bc = compute_c5(h0, n0)
    if dl - t0 > 220:
        fe = min(t0 + 70, dl - 220)
        if fe > t0 + 10:
            hf, cf = fourier_search(n0, fe, seed + 11)
            if cf < bc:
                bh, bc = hf, cf
    me = min(dl - 150, t0 + 700)
    if me < t0 + 60:
        me = dl - 20
    hh, cc = optimize_general(bh, n0, me, 2, seed)
    if cc < bc:
        bh, bc, n0 = hh, cc, len(hh)
    if time.time() < dl - 130:
        hv = perturb_variant(bh, n0, seed + 1, 0.03)
        hv, cc = optimize_general(hv, n0, min(dl - 90, time.time() + 80), 1, seed + 2)
        if cc < bc:
            bh, bc = hv, cc
    if time.time() < dl - 160:
        n2 = 400 if n0 < 400 else min(600, n0 + 100)
        if n2 != n0 and n2 < 1000:
            h2 = interp_h(bh, n0, n2)
            hh, cc = optimize_general(h2, n2, min(dl - 35, time.time() + 150), 1, seed + 3)
            if cc < bc:
                bh, bc = hh, cc
    if time.time() < dl - 2:
        ph, pc = pair_polish(bh, len(bh), dl - 1, seed + 10)
        if pc < bc:
            bh, bc = ph, pc
    bh = project_feasible(bh, len(bh))
    bc = compute_c5(bh, len(bh))
    return bh, float(bc), int(len(bh))
